//============================================================
//  KARGAS IV - Mission trigger notes
//  Required Locations: P1~P4 Start, P5 Base, P5 Home, P5 Cerebrate, P5 Area, P5 Scout, P5 Intruder,
//                      P5 Spawn1~4, P5 H1~4, P1~P4 Hunt, Player Destination, AttackPoint1, EarlyTower,
//                      MD1~MD6, BaseEntrance, BaseScan1~11, MainLoad1~2, Player Destination,
//                      P6 Base, P6 Home, P6 Area, P6 Area Ground, P6 Area Air,
//                      P6 Scout, P6 Intruder, P6 Overmind, P6 MiniBase, P6 Spawn1~4, P6 H1~8,
//                      P7 Base, P7 Home, P7 Area, P7 Area Ground, P7 Area Air,
//                      P7 Scout, P7 Intruder, P7 Overmind, P7 Spawn1~4, P7 H1~4,
//                      WestArea, Middle Area, East Area, Gas1, Gas2, Mission2 Gate1, Mission2 Gate3~4, NewRoute1~14, Waypoint1~5,
//                      Difficulty, Selector, SelectEasy, SelectNormal, SelectHard, SelectVeryHard,
//                      P6 Alert, P7 Alert, P6 Alert Ping, P7 Alert Ping,
//                      DefenseCenter1, DefenseCenter2
//
//  P6/P7 Overmind locations require preplaced invincible Overmind Cocoons
//  owned by Player 6 / Player 7. The event disables invincibility on the selected one.
//  DefenseCenter1/2 likewise require preplaced invincible Player 9 buildings.
//  Easy/Normal hand off each center to a random living player (invincibility off);
//  Normal strips Photon Cannons and Pylons at difficulty lock first; Hard+ destroy all.
//  See 15b_mission2_easy_defense_handoff.txt.
//
//  Timer rule: 12 DC tick = 1 second
//    10s = 120, 60s = 720, 90s = 1080, 3min = 2160, 3min40 = 2640, 4min = 2880, 5min = 3600, 10min = 7200
//
//  Player 8 DC roles
//    Terran Civilian      : zerg resource supply timer
//    Terran Marine        : P5 base defense cooldown
//    Terran Armory        : P6 base defense cooldown
//    Protoss Zealot       : P7 base defense cooldown
//    Terran Valkyrie      : mission phase flag (1=M1, 3=M2, 4=clear)
//    Terran Vulture       : mission2 elapsed timer
//    Protoss Probe        : P5 scout rotation index
//    Protoss Scout        : P6 scout rotation index
//    Protoss Carrier      : P7 scout rotation index
//    Terran Barracks      : P5 Hunt all-out attack timer
//    Terran Firebat       : P6 Hunt all-out attack timer
//    Protoss Arbiter      : P7 Hunt all-out attack timer
//    Terran Goliath       : P5 scout overlord cycle timer
//    Protoss Archon       : P6 scout overlord cycle timer
//    Protoss Dark Templar : P7 scout overlord cycle timer
//    Protoss Shuttle      : P1~P4 Hunt location refresh timer
//    Terran Wraith        : mission1 late-wave cooldown / P6 mission2 early alert defense recharge timer
//    Terran Battlecruiser : P5 overlord supply cycle timer
//    Protoss Dragoon      : P6 overlord supply cycle timer
//    Protoss High Templar : P7 overlord supply cycle timer
//    Terran Science Vessel: Force2(P5~P8) shared vision refresh timer
//    Terran Academy       : difficulty selection flag (0=unset, 1=easy, 2=normal, 3=hard, 4=very hard)
//    Terran Ghost         : difficulty reminder timer
//    Terran Medic         : mission2 P6 minibase destroy reward message spacing timer
//    Terran Dropship      : mission1 late-wave variant index / P7 mission2 early alert defense recharge timer
//    Protoss Corsair      : mission1 outer colony warning cooldown timer
//    Zerg Creep Colony    : mission1 P6/P7 creep colony warning counter
//    Zerg Sunken Colony   : P6 Hatchery/Lair/Hive losses for final scream assault
//    Zerg Spore Colony    : P7 Hatchery/Lair/Hive losses for final scream assault
//    Zerg Zergling        : P6 mission2 penalty assault cooldown
//    Zerg Hydralisk       : P7 mission2 penalty assault cooldown
//    Zerg Lurker          : P6 mission2 penalty assault ready flag (0=idle, 1=spawning, 2=relay, 3=warning, 4=weakened warning, 5=weakened spawning, 6=unused, 7=weakened pre-analysis queued)
//    Zerg Mutalisk        : P7 mission2 penalty assault ready flag (0=idle, 1=spawning, 2=relay, 3=warning, 4=weakened warning, 5=weakened spawning, 6=unused, 7=weakened pre-analysis queued)
//    Zerg Ultralisk       : P6 mission2 penalty assault spawn step timer / pre-roll cue pulse while ready flag is idle
//    Zerg Devourer        : P7 mission2 penalty assault spawn step timer / pre-roll cue pulse while ready flag is idle
//    Zerg Scourge         : P6 mission2 penalty assault waypoint window timer
//    Zerg Guardian        : P7 mission2 penalty assault waypoint window timer
//    Zerg Drone           : P6 mission2 penalty assault relay order cooldown
//    Zerg Defiler         : P7 mission2 penalty assault relay order cooldown
//    Protoss Reaver       : P6 mission2 penalty assault spawn stage index
//    Protoss Observer     : P7 mission2 penalty assault spawn stage index
//    Protoss Dark Archon : mission2 final scream assault preparation timer
//
//  Player 1~4 DC roles
//    Tom Kazansky (Wraith): 종족 마커(시작 3초 스냅샷) 1=Terran, 2=Zerg, 3=Protoss. 13e arsenal handoff 테란 우선 판정용. 미사용 영웅 유닛 슬롯.
//
//  Player 5 DC roles
//    Zerg Hatchery        : mission1 late-wave cooldown seed
//    Zerg Lair            : mission1 late-wave cooldown seed
//    Zerg Hive            : mission1 late-wave cooldown seed
//    Terran Marine        : P5 mission2 early Sunken Colony loss warning progress
//    Terran Firebat       : P5 mission2 early Spore Colony loss warning progress
//
//  Switch roles
//    Switch1 : defeat guard
//    Switch7 : mission1 multi scan revealers created
//    Switch8 : mission1 multi scan revealers removed
//    Switch9/10 : P6 mission2 penalty assault random selector
//    Switch11/12: P7 mission2 penalty assault random selector
//    Switch13   : mission1 P6/P7 creep colony warning display flag
//    Switch14~16: P6 mission2 penalty assault random waypoint selector
//    Switch17~19: P7 mission2 penalty assault random waypoint selector
//    Switch20/21: P6 mission2 penalty assault random Hunt selector
//    Switch22/23: P7 mission2 penalty assault random Hunt selector
//    Switch24   : P6 mission1 outer colony resource unlocked
//    Switch25   : P7 mission1 outer colony resource unlocked
//    Switch26   : mission2 first penalty assault order shuffle
//    Switch27/28: P6 mission2 penalty assault cooldown jitter
//    Switch29/30: P7 mission2 penalty assault cooldown jitter
//    Switch31   : P6 MainBase tech collapse main-assault lock
//    Switch32   : P7 MainBase tech collapse main-assault lock
//    Switch33   : P6 mission1 main assault cooldown enabled
//    Switch34   : P7 mission1 main assault cooldown enabled
//    Switch35   : P6 mission1 first tech-core assault boost used
//    Switch36   : P7 mission1 first tech-core assault boost used
//    Switch37   : P6 one-colony clear reward/message paid
//    Switch38   : P7 one-colony clear reward/message paid
//    Switch39   : mission2 overmind cocoon side selector (clear=P6, set=P7)
//    Switch40   : mission2 overmind cocoon active / reward unpaid
//    Switch41   : mission2 overmind cocoon weakened-assault pending
//    Switch42/43: P5 hunt all-out random target selector
//    Switch44/45: P6 hunt all-out random target selector
//    Switch46/47: P7 hunt all-out random target selector
//    Switch48   : P6 hunt P5 Overlord support spawn pair toggle (clear=1/3, set=2/4)
//    Switch49   : P7 hunt P5 Overlord support spawn pair toggle (clear=1/3, set=2/4)
//    Switch50   : mission2 assault setup initialized
//    Switch51   : P6 mission2 penalty assault relay pulse open
//    Switch52   : P7 mission2 penalty assault relay pulse open
//    Switch53   : P6 mission2 penalty assault spawn rally pulse open
//    Switch54   : P7 mission2 penalty assault spawn rally pulse open
//    Switch55   : P6 AI activation requested
//    Switch56   : P7 AI activation requested
//    Switch57   : P6 hunt P5 Overlord support fired this cycle
//    Switch58   : P5 Hive death ground counterattack used
//    Switch59   : P6 mission2 early alert defense charge ready
//    Switch60   : P7 mission2 early alert defense charge ready
//    Switch61/62: P6 mission2 upcoming wave selector (pre-seeded; air/ground in Switch61)
//    Switch63   : P6 mission2 detailed-analysis anchor active
//    Switch64   : P6 mission2 detailed-analysis message shown
//    Switch65/66: P7 mission2 upcoming wave selector (pre-seeded; air/ground in Switch65)
//    Switch67   : P7 mission2 detailed-analysis anchor active
//    Switch68   : P7 mission2 detailed-analysis message shown
//    Switch69   : P7 hunt P5 Overlord support fired this cycle
//    Switch71   : mission1 P6 outer colony awakening warning latch
//    Switch72   : mission1 P7 outer colony awakening warning latch
//    Switch73   : mission1 both outer colonies awakening warning latch
//    Switch74   : P6 collapsed; P7 final scream assault active
//    Switch75   : P7 collapsed; P6 final scream assault active
//    Switch76   : mission2 final scream assault intro message shown
//    Switch77   : mission2 final scream assault 30s warning shown
//    Switch78   : mission2 final scream assault launch message shown
//    Switch79   : mission2 final scream assault batch 1 spawned
//    Switch80   : mission2 final scream assault batch 2 spawned
//    Switch81   : mission2 final scream assault batch 3 spawned
//    Switch82   : mission2 final scream assault batch 4 spawned
//    Switch83   : mission2 final scream assault batch 5 spawned
//    Switch84   : P6 base defense suspended during final scream assault
//    Switch85   : P7 base defense suspended during final scream assault
//    Switch86   : mission2 final scream assault batch 6 spawned
//    Switch87   : mission2 final scream assault batch 7 spawned
//    Switch88   : mission2 final scream assault batch 8 spawned
//    Switch89   : mission2 final scream assault batch 9 spawned
//    Switch90   : mission2 final scream assault batch 10 spawned
//    Switch91   : Gas1 P5 Overmind Cocoon confirmed
//    Switch92   : Gas1 P5 Overmind Cocoon gas reward paid
//    Switch93   : Gas2 P5 Overmind Cocoon confirmed
//    Switch94   : Gas2 P5 Overmind Cocoon gas reward paid
//    Switch95   : P6 mission2 tech-structure acceleration info shown
//    Switch96   : P7 mission2 tech-structure acceleration info shown
//    Switch97~100  : P1~P4 P5 first Mutalisk warning shown
//    Switch101~104 : P1~P4 P5 first Guardian warning shown
//    Switch105~108 : P1~P4 P5 first Devourer warning shown
//    Switch109~112 : P1~P4 P5 early Sunken Colony decay warning queued
//    Switch113~116 : P1~P4 P5 early Spore Colony decay warning queued
//    Switch117     : difficulty selector spawned / selection window active
//    Switch118     : P6 mission2 first main assault has started
//    Switch119     : P7 mission2 first main assault has started
//    Switch120     : P6 mission2 difficulty cooldown boost applied for current cycle
//    Switch121     : P7 mission2 difficulty cooldown boost applied for current cycle
//    Switch122     : mission2 difficulty extra Hatcheries spawned
//    Switch123     : easy difficulty reminder queued
//    Switch124     : normal difficulty reminder queued
//    Switch125     : hard difficulty reminder queued
//    Switch126     : difficulty reminder displayed to players; clear queued reminder on P8 pass
//    Switch127     : very hard difficulty reminder queued
//    Switch128     : P6 main assault coarse (air/ground) warning rolled
//    Switch129     : P6 main assault coarse (air/ground) warning shown
//    Switch130     : P7 main assault coarse (air/ground) warning rolled
//    Switch131     : P7 main assault coarse (air/ground) warning shown
//    Switch132     : main assault first-wave seeded at game start (one-shot guard)
//    Switch134/135 : DefenseCenter1 easy-mode handoff random living-player selector (clear/clear=P1, set/clear=P2, clear/set=P3, set/set=P4)
//    Switch136     : DefenseCenter1 easy-mode handoff completed
//    Switch137/138 : DefenseCenter2 easy-mode handoff random living-player selector
//    Switch139     : DefenseCenter2 easy-mode handoff completed
//    Switch140     : mission2 P6 forward rally point revealed (Vulture >= 4080)
//    Switch141     : mission2 P6 forward rally point reward paid
//    Switch142     : mission1 arsenal facility alive gate - Barracks OR Factory at BaseEntrance (13e, prescan 1378-1380)
//    Switch143     : AttackPoint1 cerebrate destroy P5 Hunt delay applied (one-shot)
//    Switch144     : AttackPoint1 cerebrate destroy reward A (자원 +2500 / EarlyTower 성큰 전멸 / 격파 헤더) applied (one-shot)
//    Switch145/146 : mission1 arsenal handoff random selector (clear/clear=P1, set/clear=P2, clear/set=P3, set/set=P4) (13e)
//    Switch147     : mission1 arsenal handoff completed (13e)
//    Switch148     : AttackPoint1 정신체 자원 확보 안내(22:10, 호크 대사 이후) shown (one-shot)
//    Switch149     : AttackPoint1 정신체 처치 칭찬(B) shown (one-shot)
//    Switch150     : AttackPoint1 정신체 처치 보상 안내(C) shown (one-shot)
//    Switch151     : mission1 arsenal handoff personal message shown (one-shot) (13e)
//    Switch152     : P5 Cerebrate destroy reward A (작전 정보 + 자원 +1500/+500) applied (one-shot)
//    Switch153     : P5 Cerebrate destroy hawk praise(B) shown (one-shot)
//    Switch154     : P5 Cerebrate destroy reward text(C) shown (one-shot)
//
//  Map editor settings
//    Player 5/6/7 Overlord upgrades must be configured in SCMDraft:
//    Pneumatized Carapace and Antennae = Level 1 + Researched.
//
//  Note: avoid Order("...", "Men", ...) for broad combat orders.
//============================================================
// 게임 시작 브리핑
Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 2000);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, " ");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>작전 개시: 카르가스 IV");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Set Mission Objectives("<03>미션 1: 첫 전선\n\n<04>- 목표: 북서쪽 <11>주황색 저그<04> 군락 섬멸\n- 클리어 조건: 북서쪽 <11>주황색 저그<04> 군락의 해처리 / 레어 / 하이브 모두 파괴");
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Set To, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Sunken Colony", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Spore Colony", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 5", "Terran Marine", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 5", "Terran Firebat", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Ghost", Set To, 0);
	Set Switch("Switch109", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch110", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch111", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch112", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch113", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch114", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch115", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch116", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch117", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch118", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch119", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch120", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch121", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch122", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch123", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch124", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch125", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch126", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch142", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch143", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch144", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch145", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch146", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch147", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch148", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch149", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch150", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch151", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch152", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch153", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch154", clear);
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Difficulty selection
//
//  Player 8 / Terran Academy:
//    0 = unset, 1 = easy, 2 = normal, 3 = hard, 4 = very hard
//  Switch117 = selector civilian has been created.
//  Selector civilian priority: lowest active player slot receives it immediately.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 106);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 108);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", Exactly, 0);
	Switch("Switch117", Not Set);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 1", "Terran Civilian", 1, "Selector");
	Set Switch("Switch117", set);
}

Trigger("Player 2"){
Conditions:

	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Science Vessel", At least, 120);

	Run AI Script("+Vi7");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Conditions:

	Run AI Script("+Vi6");
	Run AI Script("+Vi7");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  마스터 틱 - 모든 독립 타이머 동시 증가 (12회/초)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Always();
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Civilian", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Armory", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Zealot", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Firebat", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Arbiter", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Goliath", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Archon", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dark Templar", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Battlecruiser", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dragoon", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss High Templar", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Science Vessel", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Mission 1 late-wave cooldown starts after 15 minutes.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 900);
	Command("Player 5", "Zerg Cerebrate", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Wraith", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Mission 2 main assault cooldowns advance during mission2.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Hydralisk", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// If an outer colony tech core is attacked during mission1, only that
// colony's main-assault cooldown starts advancing early.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch33", Set);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch34", Set);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Hydralisk", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Once an assault is queued, its spawn/relay timers may run in mission1 or mission2,
// but stop after the mission is complete.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Scourge", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Devourer", Add, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Guardian", Add, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  저그 시작 보너스 + AI
//  P6/P7은 미션1 동안 자원을 0으로 묶고, 미션2부터 활성화한다.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 5", Set To, 999999, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 5", Set To, 999999, gas);
}

// 미션1 동안 P6/P7은 잠복 상태로 둔다.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At most, 2);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch24", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch25", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch55", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch56", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch58", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch59", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch60", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch13", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch35", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch36", clear);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Creep Colony", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Wraith", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Dropship", Set To, 0);
	Set Resources("Player 6", Set To, 0, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 6", Set To, 0, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 7", Set To, 0, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 7", Set To, 0, gas);
}

// 각 저그 진영의 기본 AI 시작
// P5 AI는 난이도 확정(Terran Academy >= 1) 이후에만 가동한다.
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 330);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Run AI Script At Location("ZSUx", "P5 Base");
}

// P6/P7은 Switch55/56이 켜질 때 자원을 먼저 받고 AI를 시작한다.

// Keep outer colonies resource-starved during mission1 until that colony is attacked.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch24", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 6", Set To, 0, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 6", Set To, 0, gas);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch25", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 7", Set To, 0, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 7", Set To, 0, gas);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// 미션2 목표 표시 시 갈색/하얀색 군락을 깨운다.

	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 780);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch55", set);
	Set Switch("Switch56", set);
}

	Set Invincibility("Player 5", "Zerg Spore Colony", "Middle Area", enabled);
	Set Invincibility("Player 5", "Zerg Sunken Colony", "East Area", enabled);
	Set Invincibility("Player 5", "Zerg Spore Colony", "East Area", enabled);
}

}

	Set Resources("Player 6", Add, 30000, ore);

	Set Resources("Player 7", Add, 15000, gas);
	Preserve Trigger();
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Overlord supply - independent colony timers
//  P5: 20s = 240 ticks. P6/P7: 10s = 120 ticks.
//  Each colony creates 1 Overlord only while it has at most 32 Overlords
//  and at least 1 Drone.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dragoon", At least, 120);
	Command("Player 6", "Zerg Overlord", At least, 1);
	Bring("Player 6", "Zerg Overlord", "P6 MainBase", At most, 0);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dragoon", Set To, 0);
	Move Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Overlord", 3, "Anywhere", "P6 MainBase");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 7"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss High Templar", At least, 120);
	Command("Player 7", "Zerg Overlord", At least, 1);
	Bring("Player 7", "Zerg Overlord", "P7 MainBase", At most, 0);
	Switch("Switch32", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss High Templar", Set To, 0);
	Move Unit("Player 7", "Zerg Overlord", 3, "Anywhere", "P7 MainBase");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Battlecruiser", At least, 240);
	Command("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", At most, 32);
	Command("Player 5", "Zerg Drone", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Battlecruiser", Set To, 0);
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", 1, "P5 Scout");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dragoon", At least, 120);
	Command("Player 6", "Zerg Overlord", At most, 32);
	Command("Player 6", "Zerg Drone", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dragoon", Set To, 0);
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Overlord", 1, "P6 Base");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss High Templar", At least, 120);
	Command("Player 7", "Zerg Overlord", At most, 32);
	Command("Player 7", "Zerg Drone", At least, 1);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  정찰 오버로드 사이클 - 각 군락별 독립 DC (120s = 1440틱)
//  P5: Terran Goliath / Protoss Probe
//  P6: Protoss Archon / Protoss Scout
//  P7: Protoss Dark Templar / Protoss Carrier
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

//한 사이클 돌아가게 세팅
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 175);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Goliath", Set To, 2160);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Archon", Set To, 2160);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dark Templar", Set To, 2160);
}

// Index 0 → P1 Hunt
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 180);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Goliath", At least, 1440);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Probe", Exactly, 0);
Actions:
	Move Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", 1, "P5 Base", "P5 Scout");
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", "P5 Scout", "P1 Hunt", Move);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Goliath", Subtract, 1440);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Probe", Set To, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 180);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Archon", At least, 1440);

}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  멀티 위치 궤도 스캔 안내
//  11분 럴커 공습 이후, 15분 대공습 이전에 P1~P4 멀티를 2분간 정찰.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// 멀티 위치 궤도 스캔 - 12분 30초
Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 750);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Minimap Ping("P1 Multi");
	Minimap Ping("P2 Multi");
	Minimap Ping("P3 Multi");
	Minimap Ping("P4 Multi");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>노바: <04>궤도 스캔 갱신. 각 전선 외곽에 아직 확보하지 않은 자원이 있습니다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>본진 자원만으론 오래 못 버틴다. 여력이 생기면 추가 자원을 확보해라.");
}
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 750);
	Switch("Switch7", Not Set);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 1", "Map Revealer", 1, "P1 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 1", "Map Revealer", 1, "P2 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 1", "Map Revealer", 1, "P3 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 1", "Map Revealer", 1, "P4 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 2", "Map Revealer", 1, "P1 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 2", "Map Revealer", 1, "P2 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 2", "Map Revealer", 1, "P3 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 2", "Map Revealer", 1, "P4 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 3", "Map Revealer", 1, "P1 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 3", "Map Revealer", 1, "P2 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 3", "Map Revealer", 1, "P3 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 3", "Map Revealer", 1, "P4 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 4", "Map Revealer", 1, "P1 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 4", "Map Revealer", 1, "P2 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 4", "Map Revealer", 1, "P3 Multi");
	Create Unit("Player 4", "Map Revealer", 1, "P4 Multi");
	Set Switch("Switch7", set);
}
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 870);
	Switch("Switch7", Set);
	Switch("Switch8", Not Set);
Actions:
	Remove Unit("Player 1", "Map Revealer");
	Remove Unit("Player 2", "Map Revealer");
	Remove Unit("Player 3", "Map Revealer");
	Remove Unit("Player 4", "Map Revealer");
	Set Switch("Switch8", set);
}

//  웨이브 (Wait 제거 / 드론 제외 - 전투 유닛만 Order)
//  각 웨이브는 30초 전 P5 Spawn 집결을 보여준 뒤 출발 시 MainLoad1~2를 보여준다.

// 웨이브 1 - 4분 후 (정찰조)
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 210);
	Command("Player 5", "Buildings", At least, 1);
Actions:

	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn2");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn4");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", 1, "P5 Scout");
}

Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 210);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Minimap Ping("P5 Spawn1");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>노바: <04>빠른 소형 생체신호가 움직입니다.");
}
Trigger("All Players"){
Conditions:

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 1 - P5 Hive death ground counterattack
//
//  Fires once when a P5 Hive death is detected, before the late-wave
//  reward trigger consumes the P5 Zerg Hive death counter.
//  Composition: 64 Zerglings + 16 Hydralisks + 2 Ultralisks + 2 Defilers
//  = 60 Zerg supply.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Hive", At least, 1);
	Switch("Switch58", Not Set);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 2500);
	Minimap Ping("P5 Home");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>노바: <04>하이브 붕괴 확인. 잠깐... 군락 내부 신호가 역류합니다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>잔존 지상군이 한꺼번에 깨어난다. 전선을 유지해라.");
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Hive", At least, 1);
	Switch("Switch58", Not Set);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 16, "P5 Spawn1");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", 4, "P5 Spawn1");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", 1, "P5 Spawn1");
	Create Unit with Properties("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", 1, "P5 Spawn1", 1);
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 16, "P5 Spawn2");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", 4, "P5 Spawn2");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 16, "P5 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", 4, "P5 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", 1, "P5 Spawn3");
	Create Unit with Properties("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", 1, "P5 Spawn3", 1);
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 16, "P5 Spawn4");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", 4, "P5 Spawn4");
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "P5 Spawn1", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "P5 Spawn1", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", "P5 Spawn1", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", "P5 Spawn1", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "P5 Spawn2", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "P5 Spawn2", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "P5 Spawn3", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "P5 Spawn3", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", "P5 Spawn3", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", "P5 Spawn3", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "P5 Spawn4", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "P5 Spawn4", "Player Destination", Patrol);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", Set To, 720);
	Set Switch("Switch58", set);
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 1 difficulty adjustment - Normal
//
//  Player 8 / Terran Academy = 2 normal, 3 hard.
//  Normal adjustment also applies to hard unless noted otherwise.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// Wave 2 normal reinforcement - 8:00 launch group.
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 2);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 450);
	Command("Player 5", "Buildings", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn1");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn2");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", 1, "P5 Spawn4");
}

// Wave 3 normal reinforcement - 11:00 launch group.
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 2);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 630);
	Command("Player 5", "Buildings", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn1");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn2");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", 1, "P5 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", 1, "P5 Spawn4");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Lurker", 1, "P5 Spawn4");
}

// Colony destruction penalty: +5 seconds beyond easy.
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 2);
	Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Hatchery", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Wraith", Add, 60);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 2);
	Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Lair", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Wraith", Add, 60);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 2);
	Deaths("Player 5", "Zerg Hive", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Wraith", Add, 60);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Convert the easy 210s late-wave threshold into normal 200s.
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", Exactly, 2);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Wraith", At least, 2400);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Wraith", Add, 120);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//  Mission 1 difficulty adjustment - Hard
//
//  Player 8 / Terran Academy = 3 hard, 4 very hard.
//  These are extra additions on top of normal adjustment.

// Wave 1 hard reinforcement - 4:00 launch group.

	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 3);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 210);

//  늦은 웨이브 4종 변형 (15분 후, 150초마다 순환)

//  Dropship DC = 변형 인덱스 (0/1/2/3)
//
//  변형 A: 지상 스웜 (저글링/히드라/럴커 대량)

//  변형 C: 중장갑 돌파 (울트라/디파일러/히드라)

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// 군락 테크 건물 붕괴 반격 보정 (1변종 = 2520틱, 시작 보정 = 10080틱)

	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>작전 정보: <04>저그 정신체를 파괴하면 지휘체계 교란으로 공세의 규모가 감소합니다.");
}

// P5 지휘 정신체 처치 - 작전정보(A) -> 호크 칭찬(B) -> 보상 안내(C) 순서로 1줄씩 표시.
// AttackPoint1과 동일 : C->B->A 역순 배치로 사이클 분리.

// (C) 시스템 보상 안내 : Switch153(칭찬) 이후 사이클에 발동
Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch153", Set);                              //칭찬 표시 후
	Switch("Switch154", Not Set);                          //보상 안내 미표시
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>작전 보상: <04>지휘 정신체 제거. 미네랄 +1500 / 가스 +500");
	Set Switch("Switch154", set);
}

// (B) 호크 칭찬 : Switch152(처치) 이후 사이클에 발동
Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch152", Set);                              //처치 확정 후
	Switch("Switch153", Not Set);                          //칭찬 미표시
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>잘했다! 지휘 정신체를 끊었군. 이젠 방어선이 숨통 트일 거다.");
	Set Switch("Switch153", set);
}

// (A) 작전 정보 + 자원 지급 : 정신체 파괴 즉시 발동
Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch152", Not Set);                          //지휘 정신체 보상 미지급 (값싼 가드 먼저)
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);     //미션1 진행 중
	Bring("Player 5", "Zerg Cerebrate", "P5 Cerebrate", At most, 0); //지휘 정신체 파괴됨 (스캔, 마지막)
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>작전 정보: <04>저그 정신체 제거 확인. 지휘체계 교란으로 후속 공세 규모가 감소합니다.");
	Set Resources("Current Player", Add, 1500, ore);       //지휘 정신체 처치 보상 : 미네랄 +1500
	Set Resources("Current Player", Add, 500, gas);        //지휘 정신체 처치 보상 : 가스 +500
	Set Switch("Switch152", set);                          //보상 지급 완료
}

// AttackPoint1 정신체 처치 보상 - 격파 헤더(A) -> 칭찬(B) -> 보상 안내(C) 순서로 1줄씩 표시.
// 한 트리거 사이클 내에서 위 트리거가 set한 Switch를 아래 트리거가 즉시 보면 같은 프레임에
// 함께 발동해 메시지가 덮어써진다. 이를 막으려고 의존 트리거(C->B->A)를 일부러 역순 배치해
// A는 사이클 N, B는 N+1, C는 N+2에 발동 -> 표시 순서는 A,B,C로 3줄 모두 노출.

// (C) 시스템 보상/효과 안내 : Switch149(칭찬) 이후 사이클에 발동
Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch149", Set);                              //칭찬 표시 후
	Switch("Switch150", Not Set);                          //보상 안내 미표시
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>작전 보상: <04>전방 정신체 제거. 미네랄 +2500. 지휘 교란으로 전방 방어 군집이 와해됐습니다.");
	Set Switch("Switch150", set);
}

// (B) 호크의 강한 칭찬 : Switch144(격파) 이후 사이클에 발동
Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch144", Set);                              //격파 확정 후
	Switch("Switch149", Not Set);                          //칭찬 미표시
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>잘했다! 적 지휘의 급소를 정확히 짚어냈어. 자네가 해냈다.");
	Set Switch("Switch149", set);
}

// (A) 자원 +2500 + EarlyTower 성큰 전멸 + 격파 헤더 : 정신체 파괴 즉시 발동
Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch144", Not Set);                          //정신체 보상 미지급 (값싼 가드 먼저)
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);     //미션1 진행 중
	Bring("Player 5", "Zerg Cerebrate", "AttackPoint1", At most, 0); //전방 정신체 파괴됨 (스캔, 마지막)
Actions:
	Set Resources("Current Player", Add, 2500, ore);       //정신체 처치 보상 : 미네랄 +2500
	Kill Unit At Location("Player 5", "Zerg Sunken Colony", All, "EarlyTower"); //별개 보상 : EarlyTower P5 성큰 전멸
	//중앙 정렬 격파 강조 헤더
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>[ 전방 정신체 격파 ]");
	Set Switch("Switch144", set);                          //보상 지급 완료
}

// AttackPoint1 cerebrate kill - rally P5 hunt assault units toward P5 Spawn1 once.
Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch143", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 5", "Zerg Cerebrate", "AttackPoint1", At most, 0);
Actions:
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "Anywhere", "P5 Spawn1", Move);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "Anywhere", "P5 Spawn1", Move);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Lurker", "Anywhere", "P5 Spawn1", Move);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Mutalisk", "Anywhere", "P5 Spawn1", Move);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Scourge", "Anywhere", "P5 Spawn1", Move);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Queen", "Anywhere", "P5 Spawn1", Move);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", "Anywhere", "P5 Spawn1", Move);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", "Anywhere", "P5 Spawn1", Move);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Guardian", "Anywhere", "P5 Spawn1", Move);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Devourer", "Anywhere", "P5 Spawn1", Move);
	Order("Player 5", "Infested Terran", "Anywhere", "P5 Spawn1", Move);
}

// AttackPoint1 cerebrate kill - delay next P5 Hunt all-out by up to 60s (720 ticks).
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch143", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 5", "Zerg Cerebrate", "AttackPoint1", At most, 0);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", At least, 720);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch143", set);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", Subtract, 720);
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Switch("Switch143", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 5", "Zerg Cerebrate", "AttackPoint1", At most, 0);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", At most, 719);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch143", set);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", Set To, 0);
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 1890);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1892);
Actions:
	Kill Unit At Location("Player 1", "Map Revealer", 64, "P5 Cerebrate");
	Kill Unit At Location("Player 2", "Map Revealer", 64, "P5 Cerebrate");
	Kill Unit At Location("Player 3", "Map Revealer", 64, "P5 Cerebrate");
	Kill Unit At Location("Player 4", "Map Revealer", 64, "P5 Cerebrate");
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 1 - dynamic building tracking and random all-out attack
//
//  Protoss Shuttle DC = Hunt location refresh timer (10s = 120)
//  Terran Barracks DC = P5 all-out attack timer (60s = 720)
//  Switch42/43 = P5 random Hunt target selector
//    clear/clear = P1, set/clear = P2, clear/set = P3, set/set = P4.
//  If a dead player is selected, the timer is not reset and rolls again.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Command("Player 1", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Buildings", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

	Preserve Trigger();
}

	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Mutalisk", "Anywhere", "P4 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Scourge", "Anywhere", "P4 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Queen", "Anywhere", "P4 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", "Anywhere", "P4 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", "Anywhere", "P4 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Guardian", "Anywhere", "P4 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Devourer", "Anywhere", "P4 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Infested Terran", "Anywhere", "P4 Hunt", Patrol);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 1 - outer colony attack penalty
//
//  If players bypass the first colony and attack P6/P7 during mission1,
//  destroyed outer-colony structures feed resources back into that colony.
//  If either outer colony's Hatchery/Lair/Hive collapses, both P6 and P7 wake up.
//  The first tech-core collapse also primes one large assault from that colony only.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// Repeat the outer-colony intrusion warning at most once every 20 seconds.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Corsair", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Corsair", Subtract, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 1"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Corsair", Exactly, 0);
	Bring("Player 1", "Men", "P6 Alert", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Move Location("Player 1", "Men", "P6 Alert", "P6 Alert Ping");
	Minimap Ping("P6 Alert Ping");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>선봉대, 이곳이 아니다. 북서쪽 <11>주황색 저그<04> 군락으로 병력을 돌려 공격해라.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<06>경고: 미션1 진행중 <11>주황색 저그<06>를 제외한 적을 공격할 경우 해당 적이 깨어납니다.");
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Corsair", Set To, 240);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 2000);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>노바: <04>미확인 군락의 자원 흐름이 열렸습니다. 생체 반응이 급격히 증가합니다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>방금 선을 넘었다. 저 군락은 이제 우리를 향해 전력을 모을 거다.");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<06>경고: 이 군락이 깨어났습니다. 공격을 계속하면 미션2에 시작되는 공세가 미션1에 시작됩니다.");
}

	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<06>경고: <04>미션1에 <11>주황색 저그<04>가 아닌 다른 적을 공격하면 난이도가 상승합니다.");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 1 - Zerg gas store rewards
//  Switch91 : Gas1 P5 Overmind Cocoon confirmed
//  Switch92 : Gas1 P5 Overmind Cocoon gas reward paid
//  Switch93 : Gas2 P5 Overmind Cocoon confirmed
//  Switch94 : Gas2 P5 Overmind Cocoon gas reward paid
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch91", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 5", "Overmind Cocoon", "Gas1", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch91", set);
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch93", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 5", "Overmind Cocoon", "Gas2", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch93", set);
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch91", Set);
	Switch("Switch92", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 5", "Overmind Cocoon", "Gas1", At most, 0);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 1", Add, 1000, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 2", Add, 1000, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 3", Add, 1000, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 4", Add, 1000, gas);
	Set Switch("Switch92", set);
}

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch92", Set);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 2000);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>작전 보상: <04>저그 가스보관소 파괴 보상. 가스 +1000");
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  미션1 클리어 -> 미션2 즉시 진입
//  Valkyrie DC: 1 -> 3, Vulture DC 0 리셋(미션2 경과 타이머)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 8"){

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  패배 - 단계와 관계없이 미션1/2 어느 단계든 본진 파괴 시 패배.
//  Valkyrie >= 1 (게임 시작 후) + Elapsed Time 가드로 초기 전환 보호
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Command("Current Player", "Buildings", At most, 0);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 60);
	Switch("Switch1", Not Set);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\OutOfGas.wav", 2000);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>작전 실패: 전선 붕괴");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<03>호크: <04>무너진 건 방어선이지, 부대가 아니다. 교신 유지하고 잔존 병력은 후방으로 빠져라.");
	Wait(1500);
	Defeat();
}

	Set Switch("Switch114", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch115", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch116", clear);

}

	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>작전 정보: <04>주황색 저그 방어기관은 잠시 후 점막 뿌리망 고사와 함께 파괴됩니다. 지금은 적 공세에 대비해 방어선을 구축하는 것이 좋겠습니다.");
	Set Switch("Switch113", clear);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//============================================================
//  Player-claimable missile turrets
//  Preplace one Player 9 Terran Missile Turret at each MD1~MD6 location.
//============================================================

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Bring("Current Player", "Men", "MD1", At least, 1);
	Bring("Player 9", "Terran Missile Turret", "MD1", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Modify Unit Hit Points("Player 9", "Terran Missile Turret", 100, 1, "MD1"); //수리 : P9 터렛 체력 100%로 복구 후 양도
	Give Units to Player("Player 9", "Current Player", "Terran Missile Turret", 1, "MD1");
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Minimap Ping("MD1"); //수리 완료 피드백 : 해당 플레이어(Current Player) 트리거 인스턴스만 발동
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>전술 지원: <04>고장난 미사일 터렛을 수리했습니다!");
}

//============================================================
//  Mission 1 advance cue
//  자원 부족을 직접 언급하거나 진출을 명령하는 대사는 모두 제거했다.
//  진출 유도는 13e 군수시설 복구 통지와 09 AttackPoint1 정신체 큐가 단서로 대신한다.
//  (별도 대사 없음 - 플레이어가 스스로 판단하게 둔다.)
//  Switch142 : 13e 군수시설 생존 게이트 (여기서는 미사용, 시작/전환 시 clear만 유지)
//============================================================
//============================================================
//  Mission 1 - 입구 군수시설(P9 배럭/팩토리) 통제권 이양
//  기존 "입구 임시 방벽 수동 파괴"를 대체. 23분경 버려진 군수시설을
//  복구했다는 메타포로 P9 소유 배럭/팩토리 소유권을 플레이어 1명에게 이양한다.
//  무적/파괴 없음 - 순수 소유권 이양(플레이어가 시설을 띄워 입구를 열 수 있음).
//
//  종족 마커 DC : Tom Kazansky (Wraith) (P1~P4, 미사용 영웅 유닛 슬롯)
//    값 1 = 테란, 2 = 저그, 3 = 프로토스. 시작 3초 시점 일꾼으로 스냅샷.
//  Switch142     : 군수시설 생존 게이트 — P9 BaseEntrance에 배럭 OR 팩토리 중 1개 이상 (1378~1380 스냅샷)
//  Switch145/146 : 무작위 선택자 1회 추첨(clear/clear=P1, set/clear=P2, clear/set=P3, set/set=P4)
//  Switch147     : 이양 완료(가드)
//  Switch151     : 개인 메시지 1회 출력 완료(가드)
//
//  이양 대상 결정(매 사이클 재추첨 없음 - 1380초에 1회만 추첨 후 우선순위 cascade):
//    1순위) 테란 보유 플레이어 우선 : P1->P4 순으로 첫 생존 테란에게.
//    2순위) 테란이 없으면 : 1회 추첨으로 뽑힌 생존 플레이어에게(무작위).
//    3순위) 뽑힌 플레이어가 비생존이면 : P1->P4 순 첫 생존 플레이어(안전망).
//    파일 순서(테란 cascade -> 추첨 -> 생존 scan)로 한 사이클 내 우선순위가 결정됨.
//  군수시설 생존 게이트 : 23:00 직전(1378~1380) P8이 배럭/팩토리를 각각 스캔해 Switch142 set.
//    이양 조건은 Switch142 Set만 확인. Give는 배럭·팩토리 둘 다 호출(있는 유닛만 이전).
//============================================================

//----- (A) 종족 감지 : 시작 3초, 일꾼으로 종족 판정 후 마커 DC 저장 (1회성) -----//
Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 3);                          //시작 3초 후 종족 스냅샷
	Elapsed Time(At most, 5);
	Command("Current Player", "Terran SCV", At least, 1); //테란 일꾼 보유 : 테란 종족
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Current Player", "Tom Kazansky (Wraith)", Set To, 1); //종족 마커 = 1(테란)
}

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 3);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 5);
	Command("Current Player", "Zerg Drone", At least, 1); //저그 일꾼 보유 : 저그 종족
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Current Player", "Tom Kazansky (Wraith)", Set To, 2); //종족 마커 = 2(저그)
}

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 3);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 5);
	Command("Current Player", "Protoss Probe", At least, 1); //프로토스 일꾼 보유 : 프로토스 종족
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Current Player", "Tom Kazansky (Wraith)", Set To, 3); //종족 마커 = 3(프로토스)
}

//----- (A-pre) 23:00 직전 군수시설 생존 스냅샷 → Switch142 -----//
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 1378);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1380);
	Bring("Player 9", "Terran Barracks", "BaseEntrance", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch142", set);
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Elapsed Time(At least, 1378);
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1380);
	Bring("Player 9", "Terran Factory", "BaseEntrance", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch142", set);
}

//----- (B) 무작위 선택자 1회 추첨 (23분 시점, Preserve 없음 - 재추첨 안 함) -----//
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch147", Not Set);   //미이양 시에만
	Elapsed Time(At least, 1380);   //23분 시점 : 군수시설 복구 개시
	Elapsed Time(At most, 1382);    //1회성 윈도우 : 한 번만 추첨
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch145", randomize); //무작위 대상 1회 추첨
	Set Switch("Switch146", randomize);
}

//----- (C) 1순위 : 테란 보유 플레이어 우선 (P1->P4 scan, 첫 생존 테란) -----//

Conditions:
	Switch("Switch147", Not Set);                          //미이양 (값싼 가드 먼저)

	Command("Player 1", "Men", At least, 1);               //P1 생존 확인
	Switch("Switch142", Set);                              //군수시설 생존 확인 (23:00 직전 스냅샷)
Actions:
	Give Units to Player("Player 9", "Player 1", "Terran Barracks", All, "BaseEntrance"); //통제권 P1 이양
	Give Units to Player("Player 9", "Player 1", "Terran Factory", All, "BaseEntrance");
	Set Switch("Switch147", set);                          //이양 완료
}

	Elapsed Time(At least, 1380);
	Command("Player 4", "Men", At least, 1);
	Switch("Switch142", Set);                              //군수시설 생존 확인 (23:00 직전 스냅샷)
Actions:
	Give Units to Player("Player 9", "Player 4", "Terran Barracks", All, "BaseEntrance");
	Give Units to Player("Player 9", "Player 4", "Terran Factory", All, "BaseEntrance");
	Set Switch("Switch147", set);
}

//----- (F) 이양 전체 공지 : P1~P4 소유로 보내 모든 플레이어가 보게 (각자 1회) -----//
//  P8 소유 트리거의 Display/Ping/WAV는 사람 플레이어에게 안 보이므로 분리한다.
//  가드 없이 비-Preserve로 각 P1~P4가 1회씩 발동(각자 화면에 표시).
//  개인 메시지(G)는 Switch151 체인으로 이양 다음 사이클에 1회 출력(AttackPoint1 B패턴).
Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch147", Set);   //이양 완료 시 (각 플레이어 1회)
Actions:
	Minimap Ping("BaseEntrance");
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>군수 복구: <04>입구의 버려진 군수시설을 복구해 아군 지휘관 한 명에게 통제권을 이양했습니다.");
}

//----- (G) 받은 사람 개인 메시지 : Switch147(이양) 이후 사이클에 1회 출력 -----//
//  Switch151 Not Set 가드로 AttackPoint1 칭찬(B)과 동일한 사이클 분리.
Trigger("Player 1"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch147", Set);                              //이양 완료(이전 사이클)
	Switch("Switch151", Not Set);                          //개인 메시지 미표시
	Bring("Current Player", "Terran Barracks", "BaseEntrance", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>군수 복구: <04>당신이 군수시설 통제권을 확보했습니다.");
	Set Switch("Switch151", set);
}

Trigger("Player 2"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch147", Set);
	Switch("Switch151", Not Set);
	Bring("Current Player", "Terran Barracks", "BaseEntrance", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>군수 복구: <04>당신이 군수시설 통제권을 확보했습니다.");
	Set Switch("Switch151", set);
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Base defense response
//
//  P5: Terran Marine DC, P6: Terran Armory DC, P7: Protoss Zealot DC
//  P5 cooldown: 10s = 120 ticks. P6/P7 cooldown: 6s = 72 ticks.
//
//  P5 uses P5 Area for detection and P5 Intruder as the moving target.
//  P6/P7 Area Ground/Air locations are map-side filtered. Each response
//  moves the single Intruder location onto the detected unit, then orders
//  the matching defenders to patrol there. Overlords in East Area are included
//  only during that colony's home-defense response.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", At least, 120);
	Bring("Player 1", "Men", "P5 Area", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Men", "P5 Area", "P5 Intruder");
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", Set To, 540);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Hydralisk", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Lurker", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Mutalisk", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Scourge", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Ultralisk", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Guardian", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Devourer", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Queen", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Defiler", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Infested Terran", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 5"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", At least, 120);
	Bring("Player 2", "Men", "P5 Area", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 2", "Men", "P5 Area", "P5 Intruder");
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Marine", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Barracks", Set To, 540);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Zergling", "Anywhere", "P5 Intruder", Patrol);

// P6/P7 use separate Ground/Air area locations for detection. The single
// Intruder location is moved onto the detected player unit and then used
// as the patrol target. Triggers stay owned by the defending computer
// player, while P1~P4 are checked explicitly.

// P6: computer-owned defense triggers. Area Ground/Air locations filter target height.

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Armory", At least, 72);
	Switch("Switch84", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 1", "Men", "P6 Area Ground", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Men", "P6 Area Ground", "P6 Intruder");
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Hydralisk", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Lurker", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Mutalisk", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Ultralisk", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Guardian", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Queen", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Defiler", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Infested Terran", "East Area", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Move Location("Player 6", "Zerg Overlord", "P6 MainBase", "P6 Picket");
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Overlord", "P6 Picket", "P6 Intruder", Patrol);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// P6 allied-base support: after all P6 self-defense triggers so P6 home defense
// consumes the shared cooldown first when both bases are under attack.
// Only support an allied base while that base area still contains at least one building.
// Allied-base support does not issue Overlord patrol orders.

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Armory", At least, 72);
	Switch("Switch84", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 7", "Buildings", "P7 Area Ground", At least, 1);
	Bring("Player 1", "Men", "P7 Area Ground", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Men", "P7 Area Ground", "P7 Intruder");
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", "East Area", "P7 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Hydralisk", "East Area", "P7 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Lurker", "East Area", "P7 Intruder", Patrol);
	Order("Player 6", "Zerg Mutalisk", "East Area", "P7 Intruder", Patrol);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  미션 2 진입 (Valkyrie = 3, Vulture = 0 리셋 직후)
//  목표: P6 우선 공략 유도, P7은 후속 목표
//  대사 간격: 84 ticks = 7초 (한 트리거 = 한 화자 = 한 줄)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 780);
Actions:
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 2500);
	Set Mission Objectives("<03>미션 2: 갈색 군락 절단\n\n<04>- 1차 목표: 동북쪽 <15>갈색 저그<04> 군락 파괴\n- 보조 목표: 갈색 저그 <03>전방 집결지<04> 차단\n- 방어 지침: 아래쪽 전선은 방어 유지\n- 최종 목표: 잔존 저그 군락 모두 섬멸");
	Create Unit("Current Player", "Map Revealer", 1, "P6 Home");
	Minimap Ping("P6 Base");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>미션 2: 갈색 군락 절단");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<13><03>==========================================");
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<1A>노바: <04>동북쪽에 잔존 <15>갈색 저그<04> 군락을 확인했습니다. 전술 지도에 표시합니다.");
}

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 840);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  DefenseCenter1/2 방어 거점 인계 (난이도별)
//
//  쉬움(Academy DC = 1): 미션2 인트로 시점(Vulture >= 756)에 각 거점의
//    모든 P9 무적 건물을 무작위 생존 플레이어에게 통제권 이양(센터별 독립 추첨).
//    이양 시 무적 해제 → 플레이어 방어선으로 활용. 가이드 목적.
//  보통(Academy DC = 2): 난이도 확정 즉시 포토캐논·파일런만 파괴.
//    미션2 인트로에 잔존 건물을 쉬움과 동일하게 무작위 생존 플레이어에게 인계.
//  어려움+(Academy DC >= 3): 난이도 확정 즉시 두 거점 P9 건물 전량 파괴.
//
//  DefenseCenter1/2 에는 에디터에서 P9 소유 건물을 무적 상태로 미리 배치해야 함.
//
//  Switch134/135 : DefenseCenter1 인계 무작위 생존 플레이어 선택자
//  Switch136     : DefenseCenter1 인계 완료(가드)
//  Switch137/138 : DefenseCenter2 인계 무작위 생존 플레이어 선택자
//  Switch139     : DefenseCenter2 인계 완료(가드)
//    선택자 조합: clear/clear=P1, set/clear=P2, clear/set=P3, set/set=P4
//    죽은 플레이어 조합이 뽑히면 give가 발동 안 됨 → 다음 사이클 재추첨.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

//----- (A1) 보통 난이도: 난이도 확정 즉시 포토캐논·파일런만 제거 (1회성) -----//
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", Exactly, 2); //보통 난이도 확정
Actions:
	Set Invincibility("Player 9", "Protoss Photon Cannon", "DefenseCenter1", disabled); //파괴 대상만 무적 해제
	Set Invincibility("Player 9", "Protoss Pylon", "DefenseCenter1", disabled);
	Kill Unit At Location("Player 9", "Protoss Photon Cannon", All, "DefenseCenter1");
	Kill Unit At Location("Player 9", "Protoss Pylon", All, "DefenseCenter1");
	Set Invincibility("Player 9", "Protoss Photon Cannon", "DefenseCenter2", disabled);
	Set Invincibility("Player 9", "Protoss Pylon", "DefenseCenter2", disabled);
	Kill Unit At Location("Player 9", "Protoss Photon Cannon", All, "DefenseCenter2");
	Kill Unit At Location("Player 9", "Protoss Pylon", All, "DefenseCenter2");
}

//----- (A2) 어려움+ 난이도: 난이도 확정 즉시 거점 전량 파괴 (1회성) -----//
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 3); //어려움/매우 어려움 확정
Actions:
	Set Invincibility("Player 9", "Buildings", "DefenseCenter1", disabled); //파괴 전 무적 해제 : 무적 상태면 Kill 불가
	Set Invincibility("Player 9", "Buildings", "DefenseCenter2", disabled);
	Kill Unit At Location("Player 9", "Buildings", All, "DefenseCenter1"); //가이드용 거점 제거
	Kill Unit At Location("Player 9", "Buildings", All, "DefenseCenter2");
}

//----- (B) DefenseCenter1 추첨 (Preserve, 완료 전까지 매 사이클 재추첨) -----//
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 1);    //쉬움/보통 : 거점 인계
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At most, 2);
	Switch("Switch136", Not Set);                         //DC1 아직 미인계 : 인계 완료 시 단락
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);   //미션2 진행 중 : 미션 단계 플래그 3
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756); //미션2 인트로 시점 : 목표 표시 타이밍
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch134", randomize); //DC1 인계 대상 무작위 재추첨
	Set Switch("Switch135", randomize);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//----- (C) DefenseCenter1 give x4 (P1~P4) : 추첨 조합 + 생존 확인 시 1회 인계 -----//
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 1);    //쉬움/보통
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At most, 2);
	Switch("Switch136", Not Set);                         //DC1 아직 미인계
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);   //미션2 진행 중
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756); //미션2 인트로 시점
	Switch("Switch134", Not Set);                         //선택자 조합 : P1 (clear/clear)
	Switch("Switch135", Not Set);
	Command("Player 1", "Buildings", At least, 1);       //P1 생존 확인 : 죽었으면 발동X→재추첨
Actions:
	Set Invincibility("Player 9", "Buildings", "DefenseCenter1", disabled); //인계 시 무적 해제 : 방어선으로 활용
	Give Units to Player("Player 9", "Player 1", "Buildings", All, "DefenseCenter1"); //DC1 전체 건물 P1에게 통제권 이양
	Minimap Ping("DefenseCenter1");
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>방어 거점 인계: <04>DefenseCenter1 구역의 방어 건물 통제권을 이양했습니다. 무적이 해제되었으니 방어선으로 활용하십시오.");
	Set Switch("Switch136", set); //DC1 인계 완료 : roll/give 중단
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At most, 2);
	Switch("Switch136", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756);
	Switch("Switch134", Set);                             //선택자 조합 : P2 (set/clear)
	Switch("Switch135", Not Set);
	Command("Player 2", "Buildings", At least, 1);       //P2 생존 확인
Actions:
	Set Invincibility("Player 9", "Buildings", "DefenseCenter1", disabled);
	Give Units to Player("Player 9", "Player 2", "Buildings", All, "DefenseCenter1");
	Minimap Ping("DefenseCenter1");
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>방어 거점 인계: <04>DefenseCenter1 구역의 방어 건물 통제권을 이양했습니다. 무적이 해제되었으니 방어선으로 활용하십시오.");
	Set Switch("Switch136", set);
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At most, 2);
	Switch("Switch136", Not Set);

	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756);

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At most, 2);
	Switch("Switch136", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756);
	Switch("Switch134", Set);                             //선택자 조합 : P4 (set/set)
	Switch("Switch135", Set);
	Command("Player 4", "Buildings", At least, 1);       //P4 생존 확인
Actions:
	Set Invincibility("Player 9", "Buildings", "DefenseCenter1", disabled);
	Give Units to Player("Player 9", "Player 4", "Buildings", All, "DefenseCenter1");
	Minimap Ping("DefenseCenter1");
	Play WAV("sound\\Misc\\TRescue.wav", 1500);
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>방어 거점 인계: <04>DefenseCenter1 구역의 방어 건물 통제권을 이양했습니다. 무적이 해제되었으니 방어선으로 활용하십시오.");
	Set Switch("Switch136", set);
}

//----- (D) DefenseCenter2 추첨 (Preserve, 완료 전까지 매 사이클 재추첨) -----//
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 1);    //쉬움/보통
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At most, 2);
	Switch("Switch139", Not Set);                         //DC2 아직 미인계
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);   //미션2 진행 중
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At least, 756); //미션2 인트로 시점
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch137", randomize); //DC2 인계 대상 무작위 재추첨 : DC1과 독립
	Set Switch("Switch138", randomize);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission-independent - dynamic building tracking and random all-out attack
//
//  Protoss Shuttle DC = Hunt location refresh timer (10s = 120)
//  Terran Firebat DC = P6 all-out attack timer (60s = 720)
//  Protoss Arbiter DC = P7 all-out attack timer (60s = 720)
//  Switch44/45 = P6 random Hunt target selector
//  Switch46/47 = P7 random Hunt target selector
//    clear/clear = P1, set/clear = P2, clear/set = P3, set/set = P4.
//  If a dead player is selected, the timer is not reset and rolls again.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Command("Player 1", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 1", "Buildings", "Anywhere", "P1 Hunt");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Command("Player 2", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 2", "Buildings", "Anywhere", "P2 Hunt");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Command("Player 3", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 3", "Buildings", "Anywhere", "P3 Hunt");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Command("Player 4", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Move Location("Player 4", "Buildings", "Anywhere", "P4 Hunt");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Shuttle", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Firebat", At least, 720);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", At most, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch44", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch45", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch57", clear);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Command("Player 1", "Buildings", At least, 1);
	Command("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", At most, 21);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Firebat", At least, 720);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", At most, 1);
	Switch("Switch57", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch48", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch44", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch45", Not Set);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", 1, "P6 Spawn1");
	Create Unit("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", 1, "P6 Spawn3");
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", "P6 Spawn1", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Order("Player 5", "Zerg Overlord", "P6 Spawn3", "P1 Hunt", Patrol);
	Set Switch("Switch57", set);
	Set Switch("Switch48", set);
	Preserve Trigger();

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 2 - P6/P7 uncaptured-colony penalty assaults
//
//  12 DC tick = 1 second. Easy standard cooldown is 1320s = 15840 ticks (22:00).
//  Easy jitter bands are 21:30 / 22:00 / 22:30. Roll triggers subtract only the
//  cooldown they consumed, so excess progress from repeated Hatchery/Lair/Hive
//  kills stays queued. Hatchery/Lair/Hive kills add 65s worth of cooldown progress on easy.
//
//  Waves may fire during mission1 or mission2, but not after victory.
//  They are rolled randomly, then spawned across Spawn1~4 in parallel
//  timed batches to reduce blocked creation space. Spawn timers only count up;
//  separate stage counters prevent repeated or chained creation. Air-heavy
//  waves are split into smaller sub-batches. Spawned groups
//  rally to Waypoint1~5. For 60s after the final batch,
//  P6/P7 assault units reaching the selected waypoint redirect
//  toward the already selected P1~P4 Hunt location.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// Seed the first main-assault wave type at game start so the colony's air/ground
// kind is already decided before any warning fires. This also covers mission1
// penalty assaults (awakened outer colonies) and convenient testing. One-shot:
// no Preserve, so it self-disables after the first pass.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch132", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch61", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch62", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch65", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch66", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch132", set);
}

// Reset assault counters once on mission2 entry.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 12);
	Switch("Switch50", Not Set);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch26", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch27", set);
	Set Switch("Switch28", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch29", set);
	Set Switch("Switch30", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch37", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch38", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch39", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch40", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch41", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch63", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch64", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch67", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch68", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch128", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch129", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch130", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch131", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch74", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch75", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch76", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch77", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch78", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch79", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch80", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch81", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch82", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch83", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch84", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch85", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch86", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch87", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch88", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch89", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch90", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch118", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch119", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch120", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch121", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch140", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch141", clear);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Medic", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Dark Archon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Sunken Colony", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Spore Colony", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Hydralisk", Set To, 0);
}

// Clear P6 assault state only when no P6 penalty assault is already active from mission1.

	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Vulture", At most, 12);

	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Scourge", Set To, 0);

// If a colony's MainBase tech core collapses, lock that colony's main assault.

	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 6", "Zerg Hatchery", "P6 MainBase", At most, 0);
	Bring("Player 6", "Zerg Lair", "P6 MainBase", At most, 0);
	Bring("Player 6", "Zerg Hive", "P6 MainBase", At most, 0);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Switch("Switch61", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch62", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch63", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch64", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch128", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch129", clear);
	Set Switch("Switch31", set);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Switch("Switch32", Set);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch38", Not Set);
	Bring("Player 6", "Zerg Hatchery", "P6 MainBase", At most, 0);
	Bring("Player 6", "Zerg Lair", "P6 MainBase", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 1", Add, 2000, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 2", Add, 2000, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 3", Add, 2000, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 4", Add, 2000, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 1", Add, 1000, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 2", Add, 1000, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 3", Add, 1000, gas);
	Set Resources("Player 4", Add, 1000, gas);
	Set Switch("Switch38", set);
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 2 difficulty timing adjustments
//
//  First-assault timing is identical on all difficulties (5:00 / 7:00 mission2).
//  Easy keeps 21:30/22:00/22:30 repeated cooldowns (22:00 standard).
//  Normal, hard, and very hard only advance repeated cooldown progress;
//  the existing roll, warning, 10-second spawn delay, and consume
//  triggers remain unchanged.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// Repeated cooldown adjustments start only after that colony's first spawn begins.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Switch("Switch118", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", At most, 2);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch118", set);
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Switch("Switch119", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", At most, 2);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch119", set);
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Switch("Switch120", Set);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch120", clear);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);
	Switch("Switch121", Set);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch121", clear);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 2 - Overmind Cocoon pressure-relief event
//
//  Switch39 : selected cocoon side (Not Set=P6, Set=P7)
//  Switch40 : cocoon event active / reward unpaid
//  Switch41 : next main assault from selected side is weakened
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// 10:00 selection. Overmind Cocoons are preplaced as invincible buildings.
// The event removes the unselected cocoon and disables invincibility on the selected one.

	Switch("Switch41", Not Set);

}

// Weakened roll: consume the selected side's next main assault cooldown
// before the normal random roll files can consume it.
// Pre-rolls show an early analysis cue; fallback rolls preserve the selected timing if acceleration skips that window.

// P6 weakened pre-roll.

	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", Exactly, 3);

	Switch("Switch39", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch31", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", At least, 14520);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Zergling", At most, 15479);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Exactly, 0);
	Switch("Switch27", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch28", Not Set);

	Set Switch("Switch14", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch15", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch16", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch20", randomize);
	Set Switch("Switch21", randomize);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Set To, 7);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Scourge", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Reaver", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// Assault warning delay. Roll triggers show warning first, then spawn begins after 10 seconds.
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Set To, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Set To, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Scourge", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Reaver", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Devourer", At least, 120);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Mutalisk", Set To, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Devourer", Set To, 1);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Guardian", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Observer", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//
//  Mission 2 difficulty wave-size scaling
//
//  Balance note (assault scale rework):
//    Easy was cut -10% in the base wave files (10~13 / 20~23).
//    To keep Normal at the *original easy* +5% (= about -5% vs the old
//    Normal) and to leave Hard / Very Hard totals exactly unchanged,
//    the units removed from the base are moved back up into these layers:
//    half into the L2 (Normal, Academy At least 2) triggers and half into
//    the L3 (Hard, Academy At least 3) triggers, per unit type and wave.
//    L4 (Very Hard, Academy Exactly 4) is left untouched.
//
//  Net result per wave:
//    Easy      = base                  (-10% vs before)
//    Normal    = base + L2             (original easy +5%)
//    Hard      = base + L2 + L3        (unchanged total)
//    Very Hard = base + L2 + L3 + L4   (unchanged total)
//
//  These triggers do not change orders, waypoint relay timing, rewards,
//  or Overlord support.
//-----------------------------------------------------------------//

// P6 Wave 0 - Ultralisk + Zergling + Hydralisk
Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Academy", At least, 2);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 8", "Protoss Reaver", Exactly, 0);
	Switch("Switch9", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch10", Not Set);
Actions:
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Ultralisk", 2, "P6 Spawn1");
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", 8, "P6 Spawn1");
	Create Unit with Properties("Player 6", "Zerg Defiler", 1, "P6 Spawn2", 2);
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", 6, "P6 Spawn2");
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Hydralisk", 2, "P6 Spawn2");
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Ultralisk", 1, "P6 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", 7, "P6 Spawn3");
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Zergling", 6, "P6 Spawn4");
	Create Unit("Player 6", "Zerg Hydralisk", 1, "P6 Spawn4");
	Preserve Trigger();
}

// P6 패널티 강습 스폰 병력의 랜덤 랠리 목적지를 지정합니다.
// Switch53은 각 P6 스폰 묶음 직후 한 트리거 사이클 동안 열리는 랠리 펄스입니다.
Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1); //미션 진행 중일때 : 미션 진행 변수가 1 이상일때
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3); //미션 클리어 전일때 : 미션 진행 변수가 3 이하일때
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch53", clear); //P6 스폰 랠리 펄스를 닫음 : 이번 사이클에 랠리 명령이 반복 실행되지 않게 초기화
	Preserve Trigger(); //반복 트리거로 유지 : 다음 트리거 사이클에도 조건을 재검사
}

Trigger("Player 6"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At least, 1); //미션 진행 중일때 : 미션 진행 변수가 1 이상일때
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Valkyrie", At most, 3); //미션 클리어 전일때 : 미션 진행 변수가 3 이하일때
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Lurker", Exactly, 1); //P6 패널티 강습 스폰 단계일때 : 준비 플래그가 1일때
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Ultralisk", Exactly, 1); //스폰 직후 랠리 타이밍 : 스폰 단계 타이머가 1틱일때
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch53", set); //P6 스폰 랠리 펄스를 열어둠 : 이번 스폰 묶음에 웨이포인트 랠리 명령을 허용
	Preserve Trigger(); //반복 트리거로 유지 : 다음 트리거 사이클에도 조건을 재검사
}
